- This release may add analysis/UI behavior in `MainActivity.kt` and one bounded local analysis helper source. Native Vulkan query definitions, registry/header locks, generated query data, canonical report serialization, Database submission envelope, update validation, Turnip archive validation, Surface lifecycle and privacy/security ceilings remain unchanged unless separately demonstrated and contracted.
- Evidence/provenance actions expose source class, API/query path, query-group context, registry/reference relationship and an interpretation note. Registry/reference metadata is never presented as selected-device runtime support. Ordinary evidence rows remain read-only; long press or an accessibility custom action may open the evidence actions surface.
- Per-field actions may copy/share the exact displayed pair, add a bounded local watched token or open the closest local Encyclopedia token. They must not silently upload, mutate canonical report evidence or fabricate a Vulkan symbol when no suitable token exists.
- Global report search operates only on the completed current-device analysis evidence model, requires a bounded query threshold and caps visible results. Large result sets remain lazy. Encyclopedia registry search remains a separate reference evidence class.
- System ↔ Turnip A/B uses VulkanScope's existing validated driver-change and complete-collection path. Manual switches remain available. A guided A/B continuation may perform the second switch only after an explicit Run-guided-A/B user action; state is local, cancellable and never starts a network upload. Diff labels are evidence changes, not benchmark/performance/vendor-quality claims.
- Completed A/B/current sessions may be retained in app-private compressed history only. History is bounded to 8 sessions, 8 MiB uncompressed and 4 MiB compressed per retained record, uses schema `VulkanScopeAnalysisHistory1`, is deduplicated by local snapshot digest and supports explicit local deletion/baseline reuse. No history is uploaded automatically.
- Query diagnostics show only explicit query/completeness/safety evidence plus measured elapsed time around dedicated app-side probe-group calls in the current session. Timing absent from the current report/native contract remains explicitly unavailable; no invented per-query duration, retry count or start timestamp is allowed.
- Requirement Resolver may parse registry dependency/reference tokens and evaluate individual Vulkan API/extension prerequisites against authoritative runtime evidence. Boolean dependency expressions are reference metadata and must not be collapsed into a fabricated global Supported/Unsupported conclusion. Absence is negative evidence only when the relevant enumeration is authoritative; otherwise state remains Unknown.
- Custom minimum profiles are local analysis objects, schema `VulkanScopeMinimumProfile1`, bounded to 32 saved profiles and 64 rules per profile. Supported rule forms are explicit API minimum, extension requirement, exact feature boolean, numeric limit comparison and exact evidence-key comparison. Results are PASS/FAIL/UNKNOWN and do not mutate Vulkan Profiles or canonical capability evidence. Import/export is explicit SAF I/O and bounded.
- Format Explorer usage filters operate on already-collected canonical `VkFormatFeatureFlags2`/format evidence and require every selected filter to match. They do not run guessed image-format queries or infer formats from names.
- Vulkan Video Matrix is built only from the locked 47 exact-profile evidence rows, matching retained queue codec-operation evidence, bounded sampled-format rows and retained capability properties. Queue or sampled-format association never upgrades exact-profile capability state and exact-profile evidence is never promoted into codec-wide support.
- Presentation-path analysis keeps Vulkan Surface evidence and Android Display/HDR/wide-gamut evidence separate. A `COMPATIBLE EVIDENCE` label means only that both independent evidence classes contain a matching path; it is not an end-to-end presentation guarantee, color-volume measurement or swapchain success claim.
- Raw structured-report view is generated from the exact schema-v3 `technicalReport` object used by Database submission. Visible leaves are bounded/searchable and explicit JSON export is local; the export must not silently truncate or rewrite the canonical object.
- Database Compare performs an explicit user-initiated bounded GET only from the fixed official `https://vulkanscope-database-api.vulkanscope.workers.dev` origin using a validated lowercase 64-hex report id. Response is capped at 2 MiB, must contain a `technicalReport`, is compared locally and is never treated as market-share or device-ranking evidence. No new background network route is permitted.
- New analysis UI must reuse common Material 3 Expressive primitives for evidence rows, filter bars, metric/status surfaces, toggles, detail dialogs and shared scroll hints. No feature may introduce its own contradictory card/dialog/scroll design or restore a permanent indicator side lane.
- Existing TalkBack, large-text, RTL/system-font, Android TV focus/BringIntoView, Details-only activation and 0.80.15 overlay-scroll behavior remain mandatory.
- Vulkan 1.4.362/header 362, 304 Android-queryable provider extensions, 110 implemented structs, 104 query groups, 47 exact Vulkan Video profiles, Encyclopedia 842 commands / 6248 `VK_*` / 2461 `Vk*` / 476 extensions, schema 2 / technicalReport 3 and companion Database 1.0.0 use the same schema 2 / technicalReport 3 contract.
- `tools/verify_advanced_analysis_1000.py`, `tools/test_advanced_analysis_1000_state_machine.py` and `tools/test_advanced_analysis_1000_negative_mutations.py` are mandatory. The source verifier must fail against immutable 0.80.15 with version checking disabled and pass on 1.0.0. Negative mutations must reject removal of each major workflow/evidence boundary and accept an unrelated changelog wording change as false-positive control.
- `tests/golden/0.80.15_regression_contract.json` locks predecessor production/build-chain bytes. Runtime changes are restricted to `MainActivity.kt` plus the new `AdvancedAnalysis.kt`; `app/build.gradle.kts` may change only release identity.
- Android assemble/lint/unit, real-device A/B driver execution, Database live-fetch smoke, TalkBack/TV traversal and visual validation are separate evidence classes and may be called PASS only when actually executed.

## Release 1.0.1 full security, specification, correctness and usability audit requirements
- Application version is 1.0.1 with versionCode 1001. VulkanScope 1.0.0 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `2867efd5b0fdca481393d9b1fe331276ad5429b3ffb50e537decf9318cd4e8a5` with 344 files.
- The authoritative Vulkan Registry/query/header baseline remains Vulkan 1.4.362 / header 362 because the official Khronos latest Registry checked on 2026-09-07 is still 1.4.362 dated 2026-09-04. No registry/query/header bump is permitted without a newer authoritative upstream release and a complete regenerated coverage audit.
- Android compile/target API 37, AGP 9.4.0, Gradle 9.7.1, JDK 17+, Compose UI/Foundation/Animation 1.12.0 and Material 3 1.5.0-alpha27 remain the validated project/toolkit baseline. The Material 3 alpha is intentional for Expressive APIs and must not be silently replaced by a non-equivalent stable API surface.
- Local Analysis history persistence is untrusted I/O. Directory creation, compression, temporary-file write/rename, enumeration, read/decompression and deletion must fail closed without propagating storage exceptions into the Compose coroutine or process. A persistence failure must not be mislabeled as size overflow only.
- Custom minimum numeric comparisons must preserve exact integer/decimal semantics and must not use binary floating-point conversion. Values above 2^53 remain exact. Feature expectations accept only explicit true/supported/yes/1 or false/unsupported/no/0 forms; invalid tokens remain UNKNOWN. Ambiguous feature/limit suffix matches remain UNKNOWN until an exact evidence key is supplied.
- Registry dependency visualization must never turn unavailable or incomplete runtime evidence into a negative capability statement. An unparsable/missing runtime API version is Unknown. Extension absence is reported only when the authoritative enumeration for the registry-declared scope is complete; incomplete, unavailable, not-applicable and unknown enumeration states remain distinct.
- Raw technicalReport JSON flattening is a heavy local analysis operation and is created only while Raw JSON or Database Compare is active, or on an explicit export action. Other Analysis tabs must not eagerly allocate the full schema-v3 tree/leaf index.
- Evidence actions resolve exactly one watched-evidence writer. A single explicit Add to watched evidence action must not trigger duplicate state or SharedPreferences writes.
- Query Diagnostics wording must distinguish the base collector, which does not time every Vulkan query, from dedicated/on-demand probes whose app-side elapsed time is actually measured. Missing timing values are never invented.
- Shared filter/chip navigation uses responsive wrapping rather than a permanently horizontally hidden strip. Analysis destinations and other filter sets must remain discoverable across narrow screens, large text, translated labels and RTL layouts. Existing 0.80.15 transient vertical scroll-hint behavior remains unchanged.
- Production behavior changes for this release are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt` and `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/AdvancedAnalysis.kt`, plus version metadata. Native Vulkan collection, registry/generated query coverage, Vulkan Video census, report serialization, updater/Turnip security, Database submission schema, ABI set and resource ceilings remain locked to the immutable predecessor unless a separately demonstrated defect requires otherwise.
- Companion VulkanScope Database is 1.0.1. Submission schema remains 2 and technicalReport remains 3. Database 1.0.1 must preserve the 0.80.3 submission floor and the exact Vulkan 1.4.362 producer contract while explicitly recognizing VulkanScope 1.0.1 / versionCode 1001.
- `tools/verify_full_audit_1001.py`, `tools/test_full_audit_1001_state_machine.py` and `tools/test_full_audit_1001_negative_mutations.py` are mandatory. The source verifier must fail against immutable 1.0.0 with version checking disabled, pass on 1.0.1, reject representative regressions, and accept an unrelated wording change as a false-positive control.
- Final release evidence must include retained security/resource/spec/report/UI gates, clean-extract byte equality, deterministic duplicate ZIP creation and explicit Android build/runtime status. A Gradle task that cannot start because the packaging environment lacks network/cache remains NOT EXECUTED and must not be labeled PASS.

## Release 1.0.2 Material 3 Expressive filters, connectivity state, Turnip metadata and accessibility requirements
- Application version is 1.0.2 with versionCode 1002. VulkanScope 1.0.1 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `81486c4ca5c5f52b3c317a448143f67297e240d39ad715d80fa13f5d541ede51` with 349 files.
- The authoritative Vulkan Registry/query/header baseline remains Vulkan 1.4.362 / header 362 because the official Khronos latest Registry rechecked on 2026-09-07 is version 1.4.362 dated 2026-09-04. No Vulkan registry, generated query, native collector or report-semantic change is introduced.
- Current Android/Compose upstream rechecked on 2026-09-07 remains AGP 9.4.0 with API 37/JDK 17 support, Compose UI/Foundation 1.12.0 and Material 3 1.5.0-alpha27. The release does not introduce an unverified dependency bump.
- `USABILITY`: every shared chip-style capability filter uses one Material 3 Expressive horizontal carousel with visible left/right controls, selected-item auto-visibility, bounded scrolling, relative RTL behavior and no clipped fixed-width filter strip. Interactive chips and arrow controls remain standard Compose focus targets for Android TV and expose meaningful accessibility labels where the icon alone carries the action.
- `USABILITY`: flat technical count sentences such as evidence/property/query totals are replaced by responsive expressive metric cards. Metric grids collapse to one column for narrow or enlarged-text layouts, use two columns at normal phone/tablet widths and never require text truncation to fit.
- `USABILITY`: the app observes Android's default network only through `ConnectivityManager` capability state. Connected means the default network has both `NET_CAPABILITY_INTERNET` and `NET_CAPABILITY_VALIDATED`; disconnected means that validated default-network evidence is absent. No HTTP/DNS probe, report upload or third-party endpoint may be added for this status feature.
- `PRIVACY/USABILITY`: `ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE` is permitted solely for local connectivity-state observation. The transient connected/disconnected banner uses distinct green/red state treatments, distinct icons, a polite accessibility live region and bounded 4.5-second display after a state transition.
- `USABILITY/CORRECTNESS`: installed Turnip bundle details expose the selected ZIP display name, SAF URI location, source size/modified time when the provider supplies them, import time, package name/description/version/vendor/author/minApi/schema, driver version/date when actually declared, and Vulkan library name/size. Missing metadata remains explicitly unavailable and is never inferred from GPU identity or filename.
- `PRIVACY`: Turnip source-document provenance is stored only in app-private preferences and is UI-only. SAF URI/location, ZIP provenance and import source metadata must not enter schema-v3 technicalReport, TXT/HTML export, VulkanScope Database submission, Analysis history snapshots or public logs.
- Existing Turnip archive validation, path confinement, read-only native-library hardening, complete-collection mutation gate, system-driver isolation and package size/entry bounds remain mandatory and unchanged.
- System-font substitution, UTF-8/bidi content direction, large-text/display-size behavior, TalkBack semantics, heading/live-region ownership, Android TV D-pad focus/BringIntoView and portrait/landscape responsive layout remain retained release gates. No bundled font or fixed text-height workaround may be introduced.
- Production runtime changes are restricted to `MainActivity.kt`, `AndroidManifest.xml` and the three audited vector drawable additions for left navigation and connected/disconnected network state, plus version metadata. Native Vulkan collection, registry/generated data, report serialization, Database envelope, updater validation, ABI set and resource ceilings remain locked to immutable 1.0.1.
- Companion VulkanScope Database is 1.0.2. Submission schema remains 2 and technicalReport remains 3. Database 1.0.2 preserves the 0.80.3 submission floor, normalizer 16, historical report identity, D1 schema and Vulkan 1.4.362 producer/query semantics while recognizing VulkanScope 1.0.2 / versionCode 1002 as the current producer.
- `tools/verify_expressive_connectivity_turnip_1002.py`, `tools/test_expressive_connectivity_turnip_1002_state_machine.py` and `tools/test_expressive_connectivity_turnip_1002_negative_mutations.py` are mandatory. The verifier must fail immutable 1.0.1 with version checking disabled, pass 1.0.2, reject representative filter/network/Turnip/privacy/accessibility regressions and accept an unrelated wording change as a false-positive control.
- Final evidence includes all retained security/spec/report/lifecycle/resource/UI/accessibility gates, Database Worker/D1/Pages gates, strict package hygiene, clean-extract full re-verification, source/extract byte equality, two independently created byte-identical deterministic ZIPs and explicit Android build/runtime evidence class. Unexecuted Android device, TalkBack, TV and live Cloudflare tests remain `NOT EXECUTED`, never PASS.

## Release 1.0.3 Kotlin regular-expression compile-regression requirements
- Application version is 1.0.3 with versionCode 1003. VulkanScope 1.0.2 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `986ff50b41e6a13ecc08778ef55205dcb66d9c428f552415e3933ba0010c1e48` with 357 files.
- The demonstrated `:app:compileReleaseKotlin` failure is a `BUG` / `REGRESSION` / `TEST-GAP`: Kotlin rejects the ordinary-string escape sequences `\s`, `\.`, and `\d` used directly in four `Regex` patterns in `AdvancedAnalysis.kt` and `MainActivity.kt`.
- The repair uses Kotlin raw triple-quoted strings for only those four regular-expression literals so the runtime regex text remains exactly the intended `\s`, `\.`, and `\d` syntax without Kotlin string escaping. The matching/evaluation logic, evidence semantics and UI behavior remain unchanged.
- Production runtime byte changes are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/AdvancedAnalysis.kt` and `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt`, plus version metadata in `app/build.gradle.kts`. AndroidManifest, vector resources, native Vulkan collection, registry/generated data, report serialization, Turnip handling, connectivity behavior, updater validation, ABI set and security/resource ceilings remain byte-identical to immutable 1.0.2.
- Vulkan Registry/query/header remains Vulkan 1.4.362 / header 362; the authoritative Khronos latest Registry was rechecked on 2026-09-07 and remains version 1.4.362 dated 2026-09-04. No Vulkan reference/query/header change is introduced.
- Kotlin's current string documentation confirms that ordinary double-quoted strings support only defined escape sequences while raw triple-quoted strings treat backslashes as ordinary characters. No dependency or toolchain version is changed by this repair.
- Companion VulkanScope Database remains 1.0.2. Submission schema remains 2 and technicalReport remains 3; this compile-only application repair does not change the Database envelope, normalizer, D1 schema or report identity.
- `tools/verify_kotlin_regex_compile_1003.py` and `tools/test_kotlin_regex_compile_1003_negative_mutations.py` are mandatory. The verifier must fail against immutable 1.0.2 with version checking disabled, pass on 1.0.3, reject restoration of each demonstrated invalid ordinary-string regex escape, and accept an unrelated documentation wording change as the false-positive control.
- `tests/golden/1.0.2_regression_contract.json` locks immutable predecessor production/build-chain bytes. The only allowlisted production runtime changes are the two Kotlin sources containing the demonstrated compile errors; build metadata may change only versionName/versionCode.
- Android `assembleRelease`, lint, unit tests and real-device execution remain separate evidence classes. The user's supplied 1.0.2 `assembleRelease` run is recorded as FAIL at `:app:compileReleaseKotlin`; 1.0.3 may be labeled Android-build PASS only after an actual post-fix Gradle build completes successfully.


## Release 1.0.4 offline availability, Turnip driver manager and filter-edge continuity requirements
- Application version is 1.0.4 with versionCode 1004. VulkanScope 1.0.3 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `b5ed5bdfd833a9b64760af2b4dbb7d9a74440ca4e7d5e584323f2f6ad0678167` with 361 files.
- The authoritative Vulkan Registry/query/header baseline remains Vulkan 1.4.362 / header 362. Khronos Registry recheck on 2026-09-07 reports version 1.4.362 dated 2026-09-04. No registry, generated query, native Vulkan collector, report schema or capability-semantics change is introduced by this release.
- Android connectivity state continues to use only the default network and requires both `NET_CAPABILITY_INTERNET` and `NET_CAPABILITY_VALIDATED`. The status system must not issue HTTP, DNS or socket probes merely to determine connectivity.
- `BUG/USABILITY`: the transient connected/disconnected banner must retain the last non-hidden state for its exit animation. Transitioning to `HIDDEN` must never make a green Connected banner leave the screen using red Disconnected text/icon/color.
- `USABILITY`: a startup without validated internet must expose a persistent blue informational banner explaining that Vulkan inspection remains local while internet-dependent Database, web-link and update actions are unavailable. The persistent offline information banner remains present for the entire offline state rather than timing out.
- `CORRECTNESS/USABILITY`: internet-dependent actions are truly disabled while validated connectivity is absent. Database submission and public-report fetch must additionally re-check validated connectivity at execution time so a connection loss between composition and action execution fails closed. Built-in update check/download and external web/specification actions are disabled while offline.
- Network transition presentation and the persistent offline information surface must not compete with the active Vulkan collection banner. A connectivity transition that occurs while collection is active must remain pending and receive its bounded visible interval after collection no longer owns the transient status surface.
- `USABILITY`: choosing the Turnip source in Settings opens the Turnip driver manager below the source selector and must not directly launch SAF. Import is an explicit manager action.
- `CORRECTNESS/SECURITY`: the Turnip manager retains at most 10 private driver packages. Each import is bounded by the existing raw ZIP, entry-count, decompressed-total and per-file ceilings, enforces canonical path confinement, exactly one schema-v1 `meta.json`, metadata-declared non-empty Vulkan `.so`, read-only native libraries and an atomic private-storage commit. Import never auto-activates a new driver.
- Each managed Turnip package owns a fixed numbered private slot and UI-only `source.json` provenance record containing only the bounded source display name/location/size/modified time and import time. Source ZIP provenance remains absent from technicalReport, TXT/HTML export, Analysis history, Database submission and public logs.
- Existing legacy single-driver private storage may migrate once into slot 01 only when the legacy package validates. Migration must roll back the directory move if provenance persistence or preference commit fails; it must not fabricate missing legacy source metadata.
- Managed driver rows expose slot, source ZIP name, driver/package identity, declared driver version and state. Details expose source ZIP name/location/size/modified/import time plus declared driver date/package version/vendor/author/min API/schema and validated Vulkan library name/size. Missing package metadata remains explicit `Not provided`/`Not available` and is never inferred from filenames, GPU names or driver marketing names.
- Removing an active managed driver first clears the active private selection; if Turnip is currently in use, VulkanScope falls back to the System source before the next collection. Invalid/incomplete private slots remain removable and never become active.
- `USABILITY/SECURITY`: normal ZIP import remains `ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocument`. If no handler exists or picker launch fails, VulkanScope may search only permissionless app-specific storage locations for exact names `turnip_01.zip` through `turnip_10.zip`. It must not request `MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE`, invent broad shared-storage access or silently scan arbitrary user storage. Found candidates are reviewed in-app and imported only after explicit checkbox selection.
- Shared Material 3 Expressive capability-filter carousels retain left/right controls, RTL-relative navigation, selected-item visibility and TV/TalkBack behavior, and add state-dependent leading/trailing gradient edge shadows so partially clipped filters have a deliberate visual continuation cue.
- Large text, display-size scaling, portrait/landscape, RTL/system-font substitution, TalkBack semantics, Android TV focus/BringIntoView, fixed resource ceilings, report correctness, updater validation and all retained security/lifecycle contracts remain mandatory.
- Production runtime changes are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt` plus version metadata in `app/build.gradle.kts`. Native Vulkan sources, AndroidManifest permissions, registry/generated data, report serialization schema, Database endpoint/envelope, ABI set and dependency versions remain locked to immutable 1.0.3.
- Companion VulkanScope Database remains schema-compatible with this release because submission schema remains 2 and technicalReport remains 3. This application release adds no submission field or report-normalization change.
- `tools/verify_offline_turnip_manager_1004.py`, `tools/test_offline_turnip_manager_1004_state_machine.py` and `tools/test_offline_turnip_manager_1004_negative_mutations.py` are mandatory. The source verifier must fail immutable 1.0.3 with version checking disabled, pass on 1.0.4, reject representative network, manager, fallback-storage, provenance-privacy, carousel-edge and release-identity regressions, and accept an unrelated audit wording change as a false-positive control.
- `tests/golden/1.0.3_regression_contract.json` locks immutable predecessor production/build-chain bytes. Only the audited MainActivity runtime change is allowlisted; build metadata may change only versionName/versionCode.
- Final release evidence must include retained spec/security/report/lifecycle/resource/UI/accessibility gates, strict package hygiene, clean-extract full re-verification, source/extract byte equality, two independently produced byte-identical deterministic ZIPs and explicit Android build/lint/unit/device evidence classes. An unexecuted class is `NOT EXECUTED`, never PASS.

## Release 1.0.5 Kotlin default-parameter call-site compile-regression requirements
- Application version is 1.0.5 with versionCode 1005. VulkanScope 1.0.4 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `e12a7ffd3fcf40a8c6423cae19beb07a4f349abb1bed0d6cc45abbd25d9b5589` with 366 files.
- The user's Windows `:app:assembleRelease` run is the demonstrated `BUG` / `REGRESSION` / `TEST-GAP`: `:app:compileReleaseKotlin` rejects both `ExpressiveTextButton("Cancel", onDismiss)` calls because the function signature is `ExpressiveTextButton(label: String, enabled: Boolean = true, onClick: () -> Unit)` and the second positional argument binds to `enabled` rather than skipping the defaulted Boolean parameter. The compiler reports an actual `() -> Unit` where `Boolean` is expected and also reports no value for `onClick`.
- The repair is restricted to the two demonstrated call sites in `MainActivity.kt`: each Cancel action uses `ExpressiveTextButton("Cancel", onClick = onDismiss)`. The function signature, enabled-state behavior, dismissal callback identity, visual design and update workflow remain unchanged.
- Kotlin's current function documentation confirms that a defaulted parameter preceding a required parameter can be skipped only by naming the later argument, while a functional last parameter may alternatively be supplied as a trailing lambda. This repair uses the named argument to keep the existing callback reference and make the intent explicit.
- The authoritative Khronos latest Registry rechecked on 2026-09-08 remains Vulkan 1.4.362 dated 2026-09-04. Vulkan registry/header/query/generated data and native collection remain unchanged.
- Production runtime byte changes are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt`, plus version metadata in `app/build.gradle.kts`. Native Vulkan sources, AndroidManifest, resources, AdvancedAnalysis, report serialization, Database endpoint/envelope, Turnip manager, connectivity behavior, updater validation, ABI set, dependencies and resource ceilings remain byte-identical to immutable 1.0.4.
- Companion VulkanScope Database remains 1.0.2. Submission schema remains 2 and technicalReport remains 3; this compile-only application repair introduces no Database, D1, normalizer or report-identity change.
- `tools/verify_kotlin_default_parameter_compile_1005.py` and `tools/test_kotlin_default_parameter_compile_1005_negative_mutations.py` are mandatory. The verifier must fail immutable 1.0.4 with version checking disabled, pass 1.0.5, reject restoration of either positional Cancel callback call and accept an unrelated documentation wording change as the false-positive control.
- `tests/golden/1.0.4_regression_contract.json` locks immutable predecessor production/build-chain bytes. Only `MainActivity.kt` is allowlisted for the demonstrated compile fix; build metadata may change only versionName/versionCode.
- Android assemble, lint, unit tests and real-device execution remain separate evidence classes. The user's supplied 1.0.4 `assembleRelease` run is recorded as FAIL at `:app:compileReleaseKotlin`; 1.0.5 may be labeled Android-build PASS only after an actual post-fix Gradle build completes successfully.


## Release 1.0.6 driver confirmation, network transition and UI-coherence requirements
- Application version is 1.0.6 with versionCode 1006. VulkanScope 1.0.5 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `c9176c1b120074ad76cc55ecd319f9344c070e792916ff2b82f2e45de968e624` with 370 files.
- The authoritative Vulkan Registry/header/query baseline remains Vulkan 1.4.362 / header 362. The Khronos latest Registry recheck on 2026-09-08 remains version 1.4.362 dated 2026-09-04. No native Vulkan, generated query, registry, report-schema or capability-semantics change is introduced.
- `BUG/TEST-GAP`: the default-network callback must consume ordered callback evidence. `onCapabilitiesChanged` uses its supplied `NetworkCapabilities`; `onLost` explicitly publishes loss for the tracked default network. The callback must not synchronously re-query the active default network to decide the same callback transition because Android documents those synchronous callback-time queries as race-prone. API 24-25 may perform a bounded delayed fallback query after `onAvailable` returns because ordered capability delivery is guaranteed only from API 26.
- Every actual validated-network transition from connected to disconnected while the Activity is observing the default network produces a fresh red Disconnected transition event. Repeated disconnect/reconnect/disconnect cycles increment transition generation independently. A stale loss for a superseded network must not overwrite the newly tracked default network.
- Connectivity status still requires both `NET_CAPABILITY_INTERNET` and `NET_CAPABILITY_VALIDATED`, adds no HTTP/DNS/socket probe and retains the persistent blue offline information surface plus collection-banner arbitration from 1.0.4.
- `BUG/USABILITY`: tapping the Turnip source from System always opens the private manager and, when an active valid Turnip package exists, requests the actual Turnip source switch. After confirmation and source rebind, the Turnip radio/card reflects `DriverMode.TURNIP`; the selector must not remain visually stuck on System after a successful switch. With no active valid Turnip package, opening the manager must not fabricate Turnip as active.
- `CORRECTNESS/USABILITY`: every user-requested System↔Turnip source switch requires an explicit Material confirmation before `applyDriverModeChange`. Cancel leaves the active driver unchanged. The confirmation remains mandatory for source-switch requests initiated from Settings or Analysis because both routes use the same request gate.
- Activating a managed Turnip slot also requires explicit confirmation before private preferences, active-slot state or Vulkan driver mode change. Confirming activation may switch VulkanScope to Turnip and start the fresh driver-bound collection; cancel performs no activation. Removing an active Turnip slot retains its removal confirmation and explicitly discloses that Turnip will be deactivated and VulkanScope will fall back to System.
- While Vulkan report collection is active, the System/Turnip selector remains disabled and displays a yellow explanatory sentence directly below the selector stating that driver switching is locked because changing the driver mid-collection would mix evidence from different driver sessions.
- `USABILITY`: shared filter-carousel continuation treatment uses two subtle state-dependent horizontal multi-stop edge shadows with a 30 dp extent and maximum black alpha below 0.5. The 1.0.4 opaque 85% black edge overlay is forbidden. RTL-relative navigation, selected-item visibility, TalkBack and Android TV behavior remain unchanged.
- `USABILITY`: common evidence/detail dialogs render their `Close` action in the Vulkan accent container. Turnip slot `Remove` actions use the same contained accent treatment. Turnip slot `Details` actions use the exact shared Extensions `DetailAffordance` hierarchy, including its chevron and Button semantics, in both wide-table and responsive-card layouts.
- Source ZIP provenance privacy, 10-slot Turnip bounds, SAF/fallback-storage restrictions, archive safety ceilings, Database/update offline gates, report completeness, Surface/driver rebind safety, accessibility, system-font substitution and all retained security/resource/lifecycle contracts remain mandatory.
- Production runtime changes are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt` plus version metadata in `app/build.gradle.kts`. Native code, AndroidManifest, resources, AdvancedAnalysis, registry/generated bytes, report serialization, Database endpoint/envelope, ABI set, dependency pins and storage/network permissions remain immutable-1.0.5 bytes.
- Companion VulkanScope Database remains 1.0.2. Submission schema remains 2 and technicalReport remains 3; this UI/state repair introduces no Database/D1/normalizer/report-identity change.
- `tools/verify_driver_confirmation_network_ui_1006.py`, `tools/test_driver_confirmation_network_ui_1006_state_machine.py` and `tools/test_driver_confirmation_network_ui_1006_negative_mutations.py` are mandatory. The verifier must fail immutable 1.0.5 with version checking disabled, pass 1.0.6, reject representative callback-race, missing-confirmation, selector, collection-warning, harsh-edge and action-style regressions, and accept an unrelated audit wording mutation as a false-positive control.
- `tests/golden/1.0.5_regression_contract.json` locks immutable predecessor production/build-chain bytes. Only the audited `MainActivity.kt` runtime change is allowlisted; build metadata may change only versionName/versionCode.

## Release 1.0.7 Turnip manager, combined connectivity and continuation UI requirements
- Application version is 1.0.7 with versionCode 1007. VulkanScope 1.0.6 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `1ecc8ff6f371104783f4294e3d7624b9029f34079c5da502336952c0d806449f` with 375 files.
- The authoritative Khronos latest Registry rechecked on 2026-09-08 remains Vulkan 1.4.362 dated 2026-09-04. Native Vulkan, registry/header/query/generated bytes, report schemas and capability semantics remain unchanged.
- `USABILITY/CORRECTNESS`: Settings exposes only the System Vulkan driver selector in the driver-source card. The Turnip driver manager is a separate persistent section immediately below it and is not hidden behind or coupled to a Turnip source selector. Turnip becomes active only by explicitly activating a validated managed slot and confirming that activation; returning to System still uses the existing confirmation gate.
- On a platform where Turnip eligibility is unsupported or cannot yet be authoritatively established, the Turnip manager section remains visible but displays only an amber `UNAVAILABLE` explanation. Installed counters, available-slot metrics, import controls and private slot rows must not be shown while the eligibility gate is unavailable. No unsupported/unknown state may be converted into fabricated installed-driver evidence.
- Managed Turnip slot state is `ACTIVE` for the selected slot, `AVAILABLE` for a non-active validated private driver whose recorded source ZIP location remains readable, and `UNAVAILABLE` otherwise. ACTIVE uses the existing positive green state, AVAILABLE uses the existing blue availability state and UNAVAILABLE uses the existing amber unavailable state. Source reachability is evidence about the recorded import source only and does not rewrite Vulkan capability evidence or technicalReport data.
- For SAF imports, VulkanScope makes a best-effort request to retain read access to the selected OpenDocument URI so Source ZIP provenance can remain verifiable after process/activity recreation. A denied or unsupported persistable grant is not fatal to an already validated private import; it causes later source reachability to report Unavailable rather than fabricating a path.
- Turnip `Remove` actions display a trash glyph in the existing Vulkan accent contained action style. Turnip `Details` remains the exact shared Extensions `DetailAffordance` hierarchy. Common detailed-evidence dialogs display the shared info glyph in a Vulkan accent header container and their contained `Close` action includes the X glyph.
- The Extension explorer section header uses the same `ic_info` glyph as the persistent offline information surface, recolored through the normal Vulkan accent section-header treatment. This is presentation only and does not change extension evidence semantics.
- `BUG/USABILITY`: connectivity presentation is never suppressed merely because Vulkan collection is active. Initial offline+collecting, disconnect+collecting and reconnect+collecting states remain visible. A network transition banner keeps its bounded 4.5-second lifetime even during collection; after a disconnected transition expires, the persistent blue offline information surface appears if the validated network is still absent.
- Collection completeness and validated-network availability are independent action gates. Database submission explicitly presents the combined lock when both collection is incomplete and internet is unavailable; if internet returns during collection, the network lock clears immediately while the collection lock remains. After collection completes, action enablement and explanatory text are recomputed from the then-current validated-network state.
- Connectivity continues to use Android's default-network callback evidence and requires both `NET_CAPABILITY_INTERNET` and `NET_CAPABILITY_VALIDATED`. No HTTP, DNS or socket connectivity probe may be added. Android's current guidance against synchronous capability re-query inside callbacks remains binding.
- `USABILITY`: shared filter carousels reserve 60 dp horizontal content padding for 48 dp edge controls, use two 82 dp surface-colored continuation masks instead of black shadow drawing, and animate enabled/disabled arrow alpha, scale and continuation opacity over 220 ms. RTL-relative navigation, selected-item visibility, TalkBack semantics and Android TV focus behavior remain unchanged. A hard black `drawWithContent` edge shadow is forbidden.
- Production runtime byte changes are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt` and the new `app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_delete.xml`, plus version metadata in `app/build.gradle.kts`. AndroidManifest, native code, AdvancedAnalysis, registry/generated data, report serialization, Database endpoint/envelope, ABI set, dependency pins, permissions and existing safety ceilings remain immutable-1.0.6 bytes.
- Companion VulkanScope Database remains 1.0.2. Submission schema remains 2 and technicalReport remains 3; this release introduces no Database/D1/normalizer/report-identity change and Source ZIP provenance remains private app state.
- `tools/verify_turnip_connectivity_filter_dialog_1007.py`, `tools/test_turnip_connectivity_filter_dialog_1007_state_machine.py` and `tools/test_turnip_connectivity_filter_dialog_1007_negative_mutations.py` are mandatory. The verifier must fail immutable 1.0.6 with version checking disabled, pass 1.0.7, reject representative manager-visibility, source-state, combined-connectivity, carousel-animation and dialog/action regressions, and accept an unrelated changelog wording mutation as a false-positive control.
- `tests/golden/1.0.6_regression_contract.json` locks immutable predecessor production/build-chain bytes. `MainActivity.kt` is the only changed predecessor runtime path; `ic_delete.xml` is the only allowlisted runtime addition; build metadata may change only versionName/versionCode.
- Final evidence must retain all applicable earlier security/spec/report/lifecycle/UI/accessibility gates, strict package hygiene, clean-extract full re-verification, source/extract byte equality, two independently produced byte-identical deterministic ZIPs and explicit Android build/lint/unit/device evidence classes. Unexecuted classes remain `NOT EXECUTED`.

## Release 1.0.8 driver-manager, confirmation-action, external-link and no-SAF exchange requirements
- Application version is 1.0.8 with versionCode 1008. VulkanScope 1.0.7 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `9509effe7630d679167ae9544fe62d843954e22b3223c421c1fc649c3c2d0a3b` with 381 files.
- The authoritative Vulkan Registry/header/query baseline remains Vulkan 1.4.362 / VK_HEADER_VERSION 362. No native Vulkan collection, generated query catalog, Registry, report schema, Surface semantics, Vulkan Video census or capability inference is changed by this UI/storage-fallback release.
- This user-requested Settings information-architecture update supersedes only the 1.0.7 requirement that System driver selection and `Turnip driver manager` be separate cards. Settings now has one persistent `Driver manager` card containing the System source row and the Turnip managed-driver area. System is bold white and exposes a contained `Activate` action only when inactive; when active it exposes the same green `ACTIVE` state treatment used for the selected Turnip slot. Turnip remains activated only through a validated managed slot and the existing confirmation gate.
- Overview source identity is explicit: `System Vulkan driver` is bold primary white; `Turnip / third-party driver` is bold Vulkan accent red. This is presentation only and never infers driver provenance beyond the selected driver mode already held by VulkanScope.
- Turnip eligibility semantics remain evidence-driven. Unsupported or not-yet-authoritatively eligible devices expose only the amber `UNAVAILABLE` explanation and no fabricated slot/import metrics. Managed slot state remains `ACTIVE`, `AVAILABLE` or `UNAVAILABLE`; an unavailable slot exposes only Remove and renders its package/source details subdued with strikethrough. The selected Turnip slot is also removable.
- Removing a selected Turnip driver requires a confirmation that explicitly states deletion and fallback to the System Vulkan driver. Confirming clears the active private slot before deletion and uses the existing System rebind/collection path; Cancel performs no mutation.
- Every Material confirmation question in current VulkanScope UI uses the shared question-title treatment: an accent-contained `?` glyph precedes the title. Every `Cancel` action in those confirmation dialogs is an uncontained, normal-weight X-labelled action. Destructive confirmation uses a contained accent action; Turnip deletion uses `Delete` plus trash glyph and analysis-history deletion uses a contained affirmative action. The shared Close action remains contained with an X glyph and must keep icon/text vertical alignment.
- Analysis local-history `Use as baseline` is contained. History `Delete` is bold/contained with a trash glyph and never deletes before confirmation. Raw evidence `Evidence actions` is a contained icon/text action. Analysis toggle rows make only the switch control interactive; the explanatory text/row is not a second click target.
- Evidence-state colors must preserve the established distinct semantics: `SUPPORTED`/`PASS` are green, `AVAILABLE` is blue, `UNSUPPORTED`/`FAIL` are red, `UNAVAILABLE` is amber, `UNKNOWN`/`UNRESOLVED` are neutral gray, `INCOMPLETE` is purple and `NOT APPLICABLE` is neutral gray. This preserves the earlier HTML/UI semantic-color contract; a generic unknown result must never reuse the amber Unavailable color.
- External web navigation uses the shared external-link/open-in-new glyph. `Open Khronos specification` is a contained external-link action. Info destinations for GitHub and VulkanScope Database are informational rows whose trailing external-link icon button is the only clickable target; the whole row must not activate the browser. In-app navigation retains the existing chevron-right affordance.
- Storage Access Framework remains preferred for user-selected Analysis snapshots, minimum profiles, structured technicalReport export and complete TXT/HTML report export. If no SAF handler exists or picker launch fails, fallback behavior must be explicit in the UI and bounded: Analysis imports search only exact VulkanScope-named JSON files in app-specific Documents/Downloads/private exchange roots; Analysis exports write only to a canonical app-specific exchange root with an internal private fallback; complete TXT/HTML export uses the existing bounded public Downloads fallback. No `MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE`, broad arbitrary-storage scan or hidden network upload may be introduced.
- No-SAF Analysis import scans at most 64 immediate files per approved root, requires exact `VulkanScope-*` suffix families and existing payload limits, canonicalizes paths and rejects files outside the root. No-SAF exports sanitize bounded file names, canonicalize the target, synchronously flush the file descriptor and verify the final size. App-specific exchange storage may be removed on uninstall and this behavior is disclosed in the Analysis UI.
- Turnip SAF-unavailable import remains the existing permissionless exact-name `turnip_01.zip` through `turnip_10.zip` review workflow in app-specific roots. This release does not broaden Turnip storage access.
- Production runtime changes from immutable 1.0.7 are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt` plus new `ic_question.xml`, `ic_open_external.xml` and `ic_evidence.xml`. `app/build.gradle.kts` changes only versionName/versionCode. Native Vulkan sources, AndroidManifest permissions, AdvancedAnalysis, Registry/generated data, report serialization, Database endpoint/envelope, schemas, ABI set, dependency pins and existing security/resource ceilings remain predecessor bytes.
- The literal 1.0.4 and 1.0.6 source/negative UI verifiers are superseded for 1.0.8 because they require driver-card, offline-banner or edge-shadow layouts intentionally replaced by later releases. Their pure state-machine tests remain mandatory. Retained 1.0.7 connectivity, Database lock arbitration, Turnip source-provenance and continuation-mask source contracts are folded into the 1.0.8 verifier so those behaviors are still structurally gated rather than silently dropped.
- `tools/verify_driver_manager_dialog_storage_ui_1008.py`, `tools/test_driver_manager_dialog_storage_ui_1008_state_machine.py` and `tools/test_driver_manager_dialog_storage_ui_1008_negative_mutations.py` are mandatory. The verifier must fail immutable 1.0.7 with version checking disabled, pass 1.0.8, reject representative manager layout, state-color, question/Cancel/destructive action, external-link interaction, toggle hit-target, unavailable-slot and no-SAF fallback regressions, and accept an unrelated changelog wording mutation as a false-positive control.
- `tests/golden/1.0.7_regression_contract.json` locks immutable predecessor production/build-chain bytes. Deterministic ZIP creation, source-to-clean-extract byte equality, package hygiene and clean-extract re-verification remain mandatory.
- Android assemble/lint/unit, real-device visual reproduction, SAF-provider/no-provider device testing, TalkBack, TV focus and driver-switch runtime testing are separate evidence classes and may be labeled PASS only if actually executed. An unavailable Gradle distribution or network-blocked wrapper means the Android build class is `NOT EXECUTED`, not PASS.

## Release 1.0.9 detail-dialog, action-target, driver-evidence and fallback-guidance requirements

- `USABILITY`: every large action/navigation surface that ends in a right chevron is informational except for the trailing chevron control. The card/body text must not invoke the action. - `USABILITY/NETWORK`: Analysis Database report-id entry and fetch are both disabled whenever Android does not report a validated internet connection. The unavailable reason is shown in amber. Reconnection re-enables report-id entry immediately; the Fetch action additionally remains gated by valid 64-hex input and in-flight state. No new connectivity probe is introduced.
- `USABILITY/STORAGE`: every import/export action discloses its no-SAF file family and destination/search roots. Analysis snapshot import accepts only bounded `VulkanScope-*-analysis.json`; minimum-profile import accepts only bounded `VulkanScope-*-minimum.json`; fallback roots remain app-specific external `Android/data/<package>/files/Documents`, `Android/data/<package>/files/Download`, then private `files/analysis_exchange`. Analysis exports use `VulkanScope-<GPU>-analysis.json`, `VulkanScope-<profile>-minimum.json` and `VulkanScope-<GPU>-technicalReport.json` respectively in that bounded root order.
- `USABILITY/STORAGE`: Turnip no-SAF import continues to accept only `turnip_01.zip` through `turnip_10.zip` and scans only private `files/turnip_imports` plus app-specific external `Android/data/<package>/files`, `/Download` and `/Documents`. Complete TXT/HTML no-SAF export continues to write the public `Download` collection with `VulkanScope-<GPU>-report.txt/.html` or `VulkanScope-<N>-GPUs-report.txt/.html`. No broad storage scan, all-files permission or hidden upload is permitted.
- Production runtime changes from immutable 1.0.8 are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt` plus new `ic_link.xml` and `ic_watch_add.xml`. `app/build.gradle.kts` changes release identity. ## Release 1.0.13 update-action and semantic-icon refinement requirements
- Application version is 1.0.13 with versionCode 1013. VulkanScope 1.0.12 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `2d0da0fdb5a913f655e4eaab09587c65243dd97128edb387590cf744c29f83dd` with 430 files.
- The canonical Khronos Vulkan Registry was rechecked on 2026-09-09 and remains Vulkan 1.4.362 dated 2026-09-04. Vulkan registry/header/query/native collection, submission schema 2, technicalReport schema 3, Database 1.0.2 compatibility, ABI policy, dependency pins, endpoints, permissions and security/resource ceilings remain unchanged.
- `USABILITY`: the user-supplied Android-head geometry introduced in 1.0.12 remains the exact packaged local VectorDrawable geometry, but the 1.0.12 requirement to retain its original green/dark colors is explicitly superseded. The Android icon uses VulkanScope's established `#E2676A` accent body and `#351719` tonal eye/background detail so the artwork belongs to the same dark-red Material 3 Expressive icon system as neighboring sections. No generic Compose tint may flatten the two-tone artwork.
- `USABILITY`: an available application update uses a dedicated semantic update-available glyph rather than a generic information glyph. The banner `Review` control is a contained Vulkan-accent affordance and uses the same right-chevron geometry and trailing-icon-only activation pattern as the established `Details` affordance.
- `USABILITY/CORRECTNESS`: the update confirmation dialog uses a semantic update-available title glyph. Cancel remains an uncontained normal-weight text action with the X glyph. The positive download action is contained, includes the semantic download glyph and remains disabled without a validated network. Pressing the positive action continues only the existing explicit APK download/validation flow; no automatic installation or background download is introduced.
- `USABILITY`: Database-related surfaces must not reuse one generic database glyph for semantically different operations. Fetch public report uses a database-download glyph, submit complete report uses a database-upload glyph, and browse/open Database uses a database-search/browse glyph. Database comparison sections use the existing compare glyph, while Database permalink/QR uses the link glyph. The generic database cylinder remains valid for the Database section identity itself.
- The new update/database vector drawables are packaged local resources, tint-compatible, network-independent and valid Android vector XML. Their semantics are presentation-only and do not alter network routing, report payloads, capability evidence, update provenance validation or Database behavior.
- Existing 1.0.12 SAF/no-SAF fallback routing, fallback-dialog/toast behavior, Android artwork geometry, 1.0.11 active-driver exclusivity, 1.0.10 PaddingValues compile repair, accessibility/large-text/RTL/TV behavior, update security checks, validated-network gating, report integrity and native Vulkan ownership remain mandatory except for the explicitly superseded Android artwork colors.
- Retained 0.80.5 update/release-notes and 0.80.7 language/font/update-information gates must recognize the explicit 1.0.13 update-affordance supersession: 0.80.5 continues to enforce bounded release-note scrolling, TV focus and dialog geometry while accepting the contained semantic `Download update`/X-Cancel and shared Review chevron; 0.80.7 continues to enforce RTL, system-font fallback and TalkBack live-region behavior while accepting the dedicated Vulkan-accent update-available glyph instead of the superseded blue generic Info glyph.
- `tools/verify_update_semantic_icons_1013.py`, `tools/test_update_semantic_icons_1013_state_machine.py` and `tools/test_update_semantic_icons_1013_negative_mutations.py` are mandatory. The verifier must fail immutable 1.0.12 with version/release-record checks disabled and pass 1.0.13. Negative mutations must reject Android palette drift, Android geometry drift, generic update-available icon restoration, loss of contained Review/chevron semantics, loss of the dialog semantic icon, loss of X Cancel, loss of the positive download glyph/network gate, Database action-icon conflation and stale release identity, while accepting an unrelated changelog wording mutation as the false-positive control.
- `tests/golden/1.0.12_regression_contract.json` locks immutable predecessor production/build-chain bytes. Production runtime changes are restricted to `MainActivity.kt`, `ic_android.xml`, the four new semantic update/Database vector resources and version identity. Native Vulkan, AndroidManifest, AdvancedAnalysis, registry/generated assets, report serialization, Database endpoint/schema/envelope, Turnip package validation and security/resource ceilings remain immutable-1.0.12 bytes.
- Deterministic ZIP creation, strict package hygiene, source-to-clean-extract byte equality and clean-extract full quality re-verification remain mandatory. Android assemble/lint/unit and real-device visual/TalkBack/TV reproduction are separate evidence classes and may be called PASS only when actually executed.

## Release 1.0.15 system-document-picker launch-first and full-audit requirements
- Application version is 1.0.15 with versionCode 1015. VulkanScope 1.0.14 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `9badb651e92ada01c34fc7e3f5df4f65e15ae38e63d0aad72e4e229485650869` with 444 files.
- The canonical Khronos Vulkan Registry was rechecked on 2026-09-09 and remains Vulkan 1.4.362 dated 2026-09-04. Registry/header/query generation, 104 validated runtime query groups, 304 validated physical-device query extensions, 110 implemented physical-device structures, submission schema 2 and technicalReport schema 3 remain unchanged unless an independent specification delta proves otherwise.
- `BUG/CORRECTNESS`: immutable 1.0.14 can route every Turnip/Analysis/Minimum/Raw-structured/complete-report document action directly to fallback even when the device system document picker is usable. The failing-before-fix oracle is a device where the `ActivityResultContract` picker launch is valid but the preceding `PackageManager.resolveActivity(..., MATCH_DEFAULT_ONLY)` probe returns null or is filtered. Android's Storage Access Framework documentation defines `ACTION_OPEN_DOCUMENT` as opening the system file picker, while PackageManager query/resolve results are subject to package-visibility/filtering behavior on Android 11+; therefore a PackageManager result is not authoritative proof that the system picker cannot be launched.
- The 1.0.12 requirements that force a `MATCH_DEFAULT_ONLY` document-picker preflight and treat Android TV as picker-unavailable are explicitly superseded. VulkanScope must attempt the registered system `OpenDocument`/`CreateDocument` Activity Result launcher first on every supported form factor. Bounded fallback may begin only when launching the system picker itself throws `ActivityNotFoundException` or `SecurityException`.
- Picker launch handling must not use `runCatching`/`catch(Throwable)` as an availability test. Cancellation or a null result returned after a successfully opened picker is a user outcome and must not trigger fallback. Runtime failures outside the two expected launch-availability exceptions must not be silently reclassified as missing SAF.
- Turnip fallback remains bounded to `turnip_01.zip` through `turnip_10.zip` under private `files/turnip_imports` and VulkanScope app-specific files/Download/Documents roots. Analysis and Minimum fallback import remains bounded to at most 64 immediate matching files per allowed app-specific root. No recursive whole-storage scan, `MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE`, guessed public path or broad-storage permission may be introduced.
- Analysis snapshot, Minimum/Profile and raw technicalReport fallback exports retain their bounded app-specific exchange destinations and exact-path toast. Complete TXT/HTML fallback retains the public Downloads behavior allowed by the existing API-level/storage contract. User-facing fallback text must say that the system document picker could not be opened; it must not claim that SAF is globally unavailable.
- `TEST-GAP`: `tools/quality_gate.py` must execute the retained 1.0.14 compile/introduction verifier, state machine and negative-mutation suite as well as the new 1.0.15 launch-first verifier, state machine and negative-mutation suite. Retained 1.0.12 SAF tests must recognize this explicit 1.0.15 supersession while preserving bounded fallback, supplied Android icon and update-glyph requirements.
- `USABILITY`: semantic Share/QR, display-badge and navigation-motion requirements are part of 1.0.15. `Share link` uses a share glyph while `Copy link` retains the chain glyph; `Database permalink & QR` uses a QR glyph. HDR-related section headers use the existing monitor motif with a compact `HDR` badge, `Supported display modes` uses the same motif with `MODE`, and `Display ↔ Vulkan interpretation` uses the monitor motif with the existing Surface glyph overlaid in the lower-right. These visual distinctions must not alter report semantics or infer display capabilities.
- `USABILITY/PERFORMANCE`: the five primary navigation destinations animate their own icon on every explicit bottom-bar or navigation-rail click. Overview uses a home pulse, Vulkan a brief tilt/pulse, Surface a short vertical lift, Display a bounded screen-like squash/pulse, and Extensions a bounded grid-like rotation/pulse. Motion must return to the neutral icon geometry, remain finite and bounded, reuse one shared composable for bottom bar and rail, and must not start any background loop or trigger Vulkan/data collection.
- `TEST-GAP`: `tools/verify_share_display_navigation_ui_1015.py`, `tools/test_share_display_navigation_ui_1015_state_machine.py` and `tools/test_share_display_navigation_ui_1015_negative_mutations.py` are mandatory and must be part of the aggregate quality gate. They must reject semantic-icon regression, lost HDR/MODE/Surface display badges, loss of bottom/rail click animation, homogenized destination motion, unbounded animation and malformed QR resources while accepting an unrelated permitted mutation.
- `tools/verify_saf_launch_first_full_audit_1015.py`, `tools/test_saf_launch_first_full_audit_1015_state_machine.py` and `tools/test_saf_launch_first_full_audit_1015_negative_mutations.py` are mandatory. The verifier must fail immutable 1.0.14 with release/version checks disabled and pass 1.0.15. Negative mutations must reject resolver-preflight restoration, hard-coded TV fallback, missing launch-first routing in Turnip/Analysis/complete-report paths, broad exception swallowing, missing bounded fallback, false `SAF unavailable` wording, broad storage access, missing retained 1.0.14 quality-gate coverage and stale release identity, while accepting an unrelated changelog mutation as a false-positive control.
- A full application audit is mandatory for this release: manifest/exported-component and transport security, update/Database endpoint confinement, bounded allocations and report ceilings, native Vulkan/JNI ownership, report loss/completeness, storage traversal, cancellation/lifecycle behavior, generated-registry parity, compile-source gates, accessibility/large-text/RTL/TV contracts and package reproducibility must be re-executed. A finding may change production only when there is concrete evidence in one of the project defect categories; speculative refactors or dependency churn are forbidden.
- Current build/runtime pins are audited separately from Vulkan specification correctness. AGP 9.4.0, compile/target API 37, AndroidX Core 1.19.0, Activity Compose 1.13.0, Compose UI/Foundation 1.12.0 and Material3 1.5.0-alpha27 remain the validated project baseline. Kotlin plugin 2.4.10 is not upgraded merely because a newer compatible compiler exists; absent a demonstrated defect, dependency churn would violate the minimal-change rule.
- Production runtime changes from immutable 1.0.14 are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt` plus the new semantic `app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_qr.xml`; `app/build.gradle.kts` changes release identity only. Native Vulkan, AndroidManifest, AdvancedAnalysis, registry/generated assets, report serialization, Database endpoint/schema/envelope, Turnip ZIP validation and established security/resource ceilings remain immutable-1.0.14 bytes unless the full audit demonstrates a separate defect.
- `tests/golden/1.0.14_regression_contract.json` locks immutable predecessor production/build-chain bytes. Deterministic ZIP creation, strict package hygiene, source-to-clean-extract byte equality and clean-extract full applicable quality re-verification remain mandatory. Android assemble/lint/unit and real-device picker rendering/behavior are separate evidence classes and may be labeled PASS only when actually executed.

## Release 1.0.16 Info, Memory and Database compatibility requirements
- Application version is 1.0.16 with versionCode 1016. VulkanScope 1.0.15 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `d53f0ff01ab6163a5520a2b7f3a3e5a7dba05bf04039f257bc00d1ba6b316703` with 453 files.
- The canonical Vulkan Registry/header/query baseline remains Vulkan 1.4.362 / header 362. Native collection, generated registry/query data, schema 2 submission, technicalReport schema 3, normalizer-facing payload semantics, Turnip handling, updater security, ABI policy, permissions and resource ceilings remain unchanged.
- `USABILITY`: the Info/Application section header uses the exact `SCOPE` wordmark cropped only from the already shipped VulkanScope horizontal logo. It is rendered as untinted fitted artwork in the existing Vulkan-red tonal icon container and must not synthesize a replacement font or redraw the wordmark.
- `USABILITY`: the VulkanScope identity row in Application uses the already shipped `vulkanscope_logo_foreground` artwork that also supplies the adaptive launcher foreground. It is rendered untinted and fitted in the existing identity container; the generic application-list glyph is not used for the VulkanScope brand identity.
- `USABILITY`: Check for updates keeps a simple downward update arrow but removes the lower tray/short horizontal line only for that Info action. The update-confirmation Download update action retains the separate download/tray glyph and all existing validated-network and APK-verification behavior.
- `USABILITY`: Developer uses a local `</>`-style code glyph in both the section header and developer identity row. Memory heaps and Memory types use two distinct local RAM glyphs so heap storage and memory-type classification are not represented by the same symbol. Run Vulkan self-tests uses a chip/check diagnostic glyph rather than the generic laboratory flask; test execution and evidence semantics are unchanged.
- `CORRECTNESS`: VulkanScope Database 1.0.8 is the companion Database. Submission remains explicit user-initiated HTTPS POST to the fixed `/v1/reports` endpoint with schema 2 and the exact schema-v3 technicalReport. Report lookup/Analysis comparison remains explicit bounded HTTPS GET of `/v1/reports/<lowercase-64-hex-id>?compact=1` from the same fixed origin and requires a returned `technicalReport` object.
- Database transport remains fail-closed at 2 MiB without truncation or selective capability omission. No background upload, new runtime network route, endpoint configurability, sensitive identifier field, D1 migration or report-hash rewrite is introduced by this application release.
- Database 1.0.8 producer identity uses the generic 1.0.x mapping `versionCode = 1000 + patch`; VulkanScope 1.0.16 / 1016 must be accepted while mismatched 1.0.16 / 1015 must be rejected. Database normalizer 16, schema 2 / technicalReport 3 and historical report readability remain unchanged.
- `tools/verify_info_memory_database_ui_1016.py`, `tools/test_info_memory_database_ui_1016_state_machine.py` and `tools/test_info_memory_database_ui_1016_negative_mutations.py` are mandatory and must run in the aggregate quality gate. The verifier must fail against immutable 1.0.15 with release/version checks disabled, pass on 1.0.16, reject each requested icon/artwork regression and Database route/schema/ceiling drift, and accept an unrelated permitted wording mutation as the false-positive control.
- Production runtime changes from immutable 1.0.15 are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt`, the new `vulkanscope_scope_wordmark.png` crop, and five local vector drawables `ic_code.xml`, `ic_memory_heap.xml`, `ic_memory_type.xml`, `ic_self_test.xml`, `ic_check_updates.xml`; `app/build.gradle.kts` changes release identity only. Existing launcher artwork itself is immutable and reused rather than regenerated.
- `tests/golden/1.0.15_regression_contract.json` locks immutable predecessor production/build-chain bytes. Deterministic ZIP creation, strict package hygiene, source-to-clean-extract byte equality and clean-extract applicable quality re-verification remain mandatory. Android assemble/lint/unit, real-device visual rasterization and live production Database smoke are separate evidence classes and may be labeled PASS only when actually executed.

## Release 1.0.18 compile-regression and full retained-audit requirements
- Application version is 1.0.18 with versionCode 1018. VulkanScope 1.0.17 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `b6bce30aa467651b900102147573c91b3772dc1f48c6c2b91fd398ebe359034a` with 470 files.
- `BUG/REGRESSION`: `ExpressiveFilterBar` has an optional `arrowTint` parameter before its required `onSelected` callback. Call sites that pass an existing callback value rather than a trailing lambda must bind it with the named argument `onSelected = ...`; a positional callback that binds to `arrowTint` is forbidden. `PhysicalDeviceSelector` must use the compile-safe named callback binding.
- `TEST-GAP`: `tools/verify_compile_regressions.py` and the 1.0.18 release-specific verifier must reject restoration of the positional `PhysicalDeviceSelector` callback. The current-release negative-mutation suite must prove this failure class is detected.
- `SPEC-MISSING/CORRECTNESS`: the canonical Vulkan Registry/header/query baseline remains Vulkan 1.4.362 / header 362. Current Khronos upstream registry evidence dated 2026-09-04 must be checked before release; no later floating/unreleased registry state is adopted. Runtime extension names, enum names, feature support and properties remain evidence-driven and exact.
- `SECURITY`: cleartext traffic stays disabled, backup stays disabled, the isolated Vulkan probe service and FileProvider remain non-exported, Database/update origins stay fixed HTTPS roots, Database report upload stays explicit user initiated, Turnip archive validation remains bounded and canonical-path checked, and no new permission or runtime network route is introduced.
- `MEMORY/PERFORMANCE`: the isolated probe worker remains single-threaded, service-owned `Surface` objects remain released, watchdog callbacks remain removed, the executor remains shut down during teardown, native handles remain destroyed in valid dependency order, and UI recomposition must not introduce repeated native Vulkan collection. The pinned libadrenotools code executes only inside the dedicated one-shot probe process for VulkanScope's custom-driver path; process teardown bounds third-party process-lifetime allocations.
- `CRASH/CORRECTNESS`: timeout, cancellation, terminal publication, malformed-result replacement, Surface recreation and driver-surface rebind recovery gates remain mandatory. Unknown/unavailable/unsupported/not-applicable states remain distinct and no missing query may become an inferred unsupported result.
- `USABILITY/DESIGN`: all 1.0.17 semantic icon, branding, red source-filter arrow, Quick access HDR, search, JSON, Database-compare and composite Surface/Display treatments remain unchanged. Material 3 Expressive spacing, large-text wrapping, TV focus behavior and scroll/navigation affordances remain covered by retained gates.
- `DATABASE`: VulkanScope Database 1.0.8 remains the companion consumer. Explicit schema-2 POST with exact schema-v3 `technicalReport`, normalizer 16 compatibility, compact bounded GET lookup and the 2 MiB fail-closed no-truncation ceiling remain unchanged. Generic 1.0.x producer identity requires versionCode `1000 + patch`; 1.0.18/1018 is valid and 1.0.18/1017 is invalid.
- Production runtime changes from immutable 1.0.17 are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt`; `app/build.gradle.kts` changes release identity only. Native/JNI/Vulkan collection, registry generated data, resources, manifest, serialization and fixed endpoint constants remain byte-identical unless a separately demonstrated defect is recorded.
- `tests/golden/1.0.17_regression_contract.json`, `tools/verify_compile_full_audit_1018.py`, `tools/test_compile_full_audit_1018_state_machine.py` and `tools/test_compile_full_audit_1018_negative_mutations.py` are mandatory. Deterministic ZIP production, clean-extract byte equality and current-release re-verification remain mandatory. Android assemble/lint/unit and real-device tests may be labeled PASS only when actually executed.

## Release 1.0.19 semantic UI refinement requirements
- Application version is 1.0.19 with versionCode 1019. VulkanScope 1.0.18 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `3c79a6ed0041b3ec966dab4431acacc5ed7a4f9220ee9bd3c5706deeb5f4e065` with 475 files.
- Current official Khronos registry evidence checked on 2026-09-09 remains Vulkan Registry 1.4.362 published 2026-09-04. The pinned Vulkan 1.4.362 / header 362 baseline, generated query catalog, native collection, report semantics, ABI policy and Database transport remain unchanged.
- `USABILITY`: Check for updates, the Direct GitHub Updates section artwork and the direct-update status/source artwork use a simple local download glyph consisting of a downward arrow with a horizontal baseline. This supersedes only the 1.0.17 requirement that those update surfaces use the ZIP-folder/download glyph. Turnip `Import driver ZIP` continues to use the ZIP-folder/download glyph because it represents ZIP-package import rather than application update discovery.

Left/right geometry, size, motion, selection, scrolling and RTL semantics remain unchanged. Vertical up/down scroll-boundary indicators are explicitly excluded and remain unchanged. This supersedes the 1.0.17 source-filter-only red-chevron treatment.
- `USABILITY`: `Queue query safety` keeps the existing queue/list glyph as primary artwork and adds a compact shield glyph at the lower-right.
- `USABILITY`: `Export complete report` uses the existing Surface glyph as primary artwork and overlays the existing export-arrow glyph at the lower-right.
- `USABILITY`: `Export TXT` keeps the existing text-document glyph and adds a compact lower-right `TXT` badge. `Export HTML` keeps the existing HTML/code glyph and adds a compact lower-right `HTML` badge. Action behavior, export completeness gates, SAF-first flow and fallback storage semantics remain unchanged.
- `USABILITY`: `Explore` uses a local compass glyph. Quick access retains its existing home glyph.
- `REGRESSION`: the compile-safe `ExpressiveFilterBar` signature and the named `PhysicalDeviceSelector` binding `onSelected = onSelected` remain intact even though horizontal carousel chevron color is now globally white.
- `SECURITY/CORRECTNESS/MEMORY/PERFORMANCE`: no new permission, runtime network route, native/JNI query, Vulkan handle ownership change, report field, Database endpoint, automatic upload, background APK install path, repeated native recomposition query or long-lived allocation is introduced. Cleartext traffic and backup remain disabled; probe/service/file-provider confinement and teardown invariants remain retained.
- `DATABASE`: VulkanScope Database 1.0.8 remains the companion consumer. Schema 2 / schema-v3 `technicalReport`, normalizer 16, compact bounded lookup and the 2 MiB fail-closed no-truncation ceiling remain unchanged. Generic 1.0.x producer identity requires versionCode `1000 + patch`; 1.0.19 / 1019 is valid and mismatched codes are rejected.
- `TEST-GAP`: `tools/verify_semantic_ui_refinement_1019.py`, `tools/test_semantic_ui_refinement_1019_state_machine.py` and `tools/test_semantic_ui_refinement_1019_negative_mutations.py` are mandatory current-release gates. No automatic/background report upload is introduced.

## Release 1.3.2 persistent update-transfer dialog requirements
- `USABILITY`: after the existing explicit `Download update` confirmation, the same modal update flow transitions into a persistent transfer-state surface instead of immediately hiding the flow and auto-opening Android's package installer. Active transfer states expose connection status, current transfer speed, downloaded byte/progress information and a bounded scrollable monospace live log.
- `USABILITY/CORRECTNESS`: the transfer state machine distinguishes CONNECTING, DOWNLOADING, PAUSED, VERIFYING, COMPLETED, CANCELED and FAILED. Active transfer state must not expose a Close action. COMPLETED, CANCELED and FAILED are terminal and may expose Close.
- `USABILITY/CORRECTNESS`: Pause is explicit and cooperative. Resume is an uncontained text action with no icon. Cancel uses a contained X action. Requesting Cancel first puts the transfer into PAUSED and then opens a question-glyph confirmation. Dismissing that confirmation resumes the same transfer; confirming it cancels the active HTTP call and records cancellation in the live log.
- `USABILITY`: successful verification renders a check-glyph `Update downloaded` terminal state. The verified APK remains available until the user explicitly chooses the contained `Install` action or closes the terminal transfer surface. Install reuses the existing update-download glyph requested by the UI contract. Close remains an uncontained X action.
- `CORRECTNESS/SECURITY`: 1.3.2 supersedes the predecessor behavior that immediately invoked `requestPackageInstall` after successful download validation. Package installation now requires a second explicit user action after verification. Official GitHub release URL validation, HTTPS-only transport, 256 MiB download ceiling, private-cache confinement, APK parseability, package identity, signing-certificate compatibility, monotonically newer versionCode/versionName checks and FileProvider installer handoff remain mandatory.
- `RESOURCE/PERFORMANCE`: live transfer logs are bounded to 120 entries. Transfer progress publication is rate-limited and must not create an unbounded Compose collection or per-byte allocation. Partial `.part` files are removed on failure, cancellation or coroutine teardown.
- `NETWORK`: no new endpoint, background download, silent install, redirect relaxation, permission or updater provenance source is introduced. Disabling Direct GitHub updates still cancels the active metadata/download calls and coroutines and clears pending transfer/installer state.
- Production runtime changes from immutable 1.3.1 are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt`; `app/build.gradle.kts` changes release identity only. Filter/HDR, license, storage, native/JNI/Vulkan collection, report serialization, Database behavior, AndroidManifest, ABI set and dependency pins remain predecessor bytes.
- `tools/verify_release_1302.py`, `tools/test_release_1302_state_machine.py`, `tools/test_release_1302_negative_mutations.py` and `tools/verify_release_1302_regression.py` are the targeted 1.3.2 gates. The source verifier must fail immutable 1.3.1 with version checking disabled, pass 1.3.2, reject representative pause/resume/cancel/terminal-state/log/security regressions and accept an unrelated permitted wording mutation as a false-positive control.
