## Release 1.3.4 in-app Turnip file manager requirements
- Application version is 1.3.4 with versionCode 1304. VulkanScope 1.3.3 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `6d31ae02caada9223dfd867734e117e4ade2fd683528a9b9c559ed5b4b8b7e52` with 543 files.
- `USER-DIRECTED/STORAGE/USABILITY`: this release adds only the Turnip ZIP storage frontend that 1.3.3 intentionally left unavailable. SAF remains removed. Analysis snapshot, minimum-profile, raw technicalReport and complete TXT/HTML filesystem export remain unchanged from 1.3.3 and receive no new storage transport in 1.3.4.
- Android 11+ shared-storage browsing uses the platform all-files special-access model because the explicitly requested in-app browser must traverse user-visible shared-storage folders without SAF. The manifest declares `android.permission.MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE`; access state is checked with `Environment.isExternalStorageManager()`. The app-specific `ACTION_MANAGE_APP_ALL_FILES_ACCESS_PERMISSION` settings surface is requested only after the user explicitly presses Import driver ZIP, with the general all-files settings page used only when the app-specific settings activity cannot be launched. Legacy READ/WRITE external-storage permissions remain forbidden.
- Broad storage access is transport authority only, not a broad data browser. VulkanScope's file-manager UI is confined to the canonical primary shared-storage root, rejects canonical traversal/symlink escape, lists readable folders, and hides every ordinary file. The only files rendered are `.zip` archives that pass bounded Turnip pre-validation.
- Pre-validation is security-relevant and must match the existing importer ceilings: at most 2048 ZIP entries, at most 64 MiB decompressed total, at most 32 MiB per file, at most 96 MiB bounded compressed input, exactly one bounded `meta.json`, schemaVersion 1, a safe metadata-declared Vulkan `.so` name and exactly one non-empty matching library. Unsafe, duplicate or traversing archive paths never become selectable.
- Pre-validation is not installation trust. Every selected archive is re-opened and pre-validated immediately before import and then passes the existing `installDriverBundleIo` extraction/schema/path/size/native-library checks. Existing private Turnip installation, atomic slot commit, activation/removal, collection gating and driver runtime behavior remain intact.
- File-manager scanning and ZIP inspection execute on `Dispatchers.IO`; the Compose/UI thread performs no archive parsing. A single folder scan is bounded to 4096 immediate entries and at most 256 candidate ZIP inspections. The UI states when a bound is reached instead of silently broadening scope.
- UI uses Material 3 Expressive presentation. It provides an up/parent action confined to the approved root, yellow folder icons, the existing official Mesa logo for validated Turnip ZIPs, green validated-package naming, modified date and ZIP size, searchable current-folder results, List and Compact view modes, and an Info action exposing available package metadata and validation details.
- Only validated Turnip ZIP rows have selection checkboxes; folders never have checkboxes. Selection is capped at the remaining private Turnip slot count and never exceeds the established 10-slot manager maximum. The header updates selected and remaining counts live. Selected files may span visited folders, but import revalidates every path and package before consuming a slot.
- If all-files access is not granted after returning from system settings, the Import driver ZIP action transitions to a visible X / Permission denied state for three seconds and no filesystem scan starts.
- Google Play distribution policy for `MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` is an external publication constraint: this release makes no claim that the permission is Play-approved for VulkanScope. Release engineering must document that Play distribution can require a restricted-permission declaration/review. This does not weaken the explicit user-directed local file-manager implementation.
- Database submission, updater, Vulkan/native/JNI collection, report schemas, registry/generated data, ABI set and dependency pins remain predecessor behavior/bytes. No new network endpoint or background storage scan is introduced.
- Production runtime changes from immutable 1.3.3 are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt`, `app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml` and the new semantic folder vector `app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_folder.xml`; `app/build.gradle.kts` changes release identity only. The build-chain may update `tools/quality_gate.py` only to select the targeted 1.3.4 gates and keep superseded SAF/fallback gates disabled.
- `tests/golden/1.3.3_file_manager_contract.json`, `tools/verify_release_1304.py`, `tools/test_release_1304_state_machine.py`, `tools/test_release_1304_negative_mutations.py` and `tools/verify_release_1304_regression.py` are the targeted 1.3.4 evidence. The source verifier must fail immutable 1.3.3 with version checking disabled, pass 1.3.4, reject representative traversal/validation/permission/slot-limit regressions and accept an unrelated permitted wording mutation as a false-positive control.
- Per the user's explicit release instruction, the historical full gate chain is not rerun locally. Only the targeted 1.3.4 file-manager gates, directly relevant Turnip importer regression/source-boundary/package-hygiene checks, and an Android Kotlin/build attempt are required locally. Any build/lint/unit class that cannot execute is `NOT EXECUTED`, never PASS.
- Deterministic ZIP creation, clean extraction, source-to-extract byte equality, strict package hygiene and clean-extract rerun of the targeted 1.3.4 gates remain mandatory.

## Release 1.3.5 full correctness, security, performance and UX audit requirements
- Application version is 1.3.5 with versionCode 1305. VulkanScope 1.3.4 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `d6e3f38cade5010c4ed537b287d08edc9e146d1e2d801bd29ec50a95065c0a6c` with 549 files.
- Current upstream/specification audit is anchored to the published Vulkan 1.4.362 baseline dated 2026-09-04. The checked-in Vulkan registry/header lock already targets 1.4.362 and is retained; floating or newer unreleased registry state is not adopted. Android compile/target remains API 37 and the existing Android 11+ all-files special-access model remains unchanged.
- `BUG/BUILD`: immutable 1.3.4 contains a duplicate local `registryCoverage` declaration in `InfoPage`; 1.3.5 removes the duplicate. This is a demonstrated Kotlin compile blocker even though the Android Gradle build class may remain unexecutable in an offline environment.
- `BUG/PERFORMANCE/USABILITY`: immutable 1.3.4 does not own Turnip file-manager directory scans with a job/generation token, does not cooperatively cancel long ZIP/directory scans and permits stale asynchronous completion to remain architecturally possible. 1.3.5 owns exactly one scan job/generation, rejects navigation while loading, invalidates/cancels work on close/destroy, checks coroutine cancellation during bounded directory/ZIP loops and suppresses stale completion. Cancellation is rethrown rather than swallowed by generic validation failure handling.
- `BUG/USABILITY`: when a new folder scan begins, old folder/package rows must be cleared so a failed target scan cannot display the previous directory's contents under the new path.
- `USABILITY/ACCESSIBILITY`: Turnip folder rows and the single-filter selector use standard enabled `clickable` button semantics rather than raw tap-only pointer handlers. This supplies the Material press/indication effect and an accessibility click action. The Evidence long-press row retains its intentional custom scale/highlight animation and accessibility custom action.
- `USABILITY`: the Turnip selected/remaining plus List/Compact header uses the established expanded-text layout policy at fontScale >= 1.3 or screen width < 360 dp so the control row does not rely on a single horizontal fit.
- `PERFORMANCE`: QR matrix generation is no longer performed synchronously during Compose composition. ZXing encoding executes on `Dispatchers.Default` through Compose-produced asynchronous state; drawing remains on the Compose canvas. Dependency pins are not changed solely for this repair.
- `BUG/LIFECYCLE`: Turnip multi-package import must never convert coroutine cancellation into an ordinary package failure. `CancellationException` from pre-import validation or private-slot installation is rethrown so Activity destruction cannot continue stale UI/toast publication after cancellation; ordinary package failures remain user-visible and bounded.
- `SECURITY/MEMORY`: 1.3.4 Turnip archive ceilings, canonical-root/symlink/traversal rejection, pre-import revalidation, existing private-slot installer validation, all-files permission timing, no-SAF contract, update/Database security, manifest export/cleartext/backup hardening, bounded probe/native ownership and activity/service cleanup remain mandatory. No demonstrated security defect requires weakening or broadening these controls.
- `SPEC-MISSING` is not established for the locked Vulkan query baseline: current published Vulkan 1.4.362 matches the project's 1.4.362 lock. Native/JNI Vulkan query code, registry/generated artifacts, report schemas, ABI set and dependency versions therefore remain production bytes from 1.3.4.
- `TEST-GAP`: historical storage/UI gates that require superseded SAF behavior or exact pre-1.3.4 UI source strings are documentary, not executable 1.3.5 requirements. `tools/quality_gate.py` must route 1.3.5 to the current full-audit gate set instead of manufacturing regressions to satisfy stale historical string checks. The current gate set still executes locked regression/spec/Vulkan, compile-static, probe/resource-lifecycle, report/profile/video/security and 1.3.5 release contracts.
- `TEST-GAP/PACKAGING`: the 1.3.5 source census must include every newly added current-release verifier/golden artifact in `files.txt`; strict source/package reproducibility must reject any omitted or extra file before release packaging.
- Production runtime changes from immutable 1.3.4 are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt` and `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/VulkanQrCode.kt`; `app/build.gradle.kts` changes release identity only. AndroidManifest, native/JNI sources, registry/generated data, resources and dependencies remain predecessor bytes.
- `tests/golden/1.3.4_regression_contract.json`, `tools/verify_release_1305.py`, `tools/test_release_1305_state_machine.py`, `tools/test_release_1305_negative_mutations.py` and `tools/verify_release_1305_regression.py` are mandatory current-release evidence. The source verifier must fail immutable 1.3.4 with version checking disabled, pass 1.3.5, reject representative compile/race/cancellation/effect/responsive/QR regressions and accept an unrelated permitted wording mutation as a false-positive control.
- Full-audit static/source gates do not substitute for Android compilation, lint/unit, real-device visual/TalkBack/TV testing, Vulkan Validation Layers or heap/sanitizer/profiler evidence. Any class that cannot execute is `NOT EXECUTED`, never PASS. A static memory-lifetime review may state that no known leak was found, but may not be represented as a profiler PASS.
- Deterministic dual-ZIP reproduction, strict package hygiene, clean extraction, full source-to-extract byte equality, current applicable quality-gate rerun and 1.3.5 targeted gate rerun on the clean extract remain mandatory.

## Release 1.3.6 in-app import/export storage completion requirements
- Application version is 1.3.6 with versionCode 1306. VulkanScope 1.3.5 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `00d8bc56012e910f4440f890561caf68e5cc934ac2876d35ccfc29e7ad342794` with 554 files.
- `USER-DIRECTED/STORAGE`: this release completes the previously deferred non-Turnip storage exchange. It supersedes only the 1.3.4 clause that Analysis snapshot, minimum-profile, raw technicalReport and TXT/HTML storage transport remain unavailable. VulkanScope-owned SAF and SAF fallback paths remain removed; no `OpenDocument`, `CreateDocument`, persistable document URI or Downloads fallback may return.
- `PERMISSION/UX`: shared-storage access continues to use the already-approved Android all-files special-access model (`MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` / `Environment.isExternalStorageManager()`). The request is initiated only by an explicit import/export action. A denied return produces the established animated X / Permission denied feedback for 3 seconds; no startup/background permission request is added.
- `SECURITY/STORAGE`: the in-app shared-storage browser is confined to the canonical primary shared-storage root, rejects symlink/path escape, bounds one directory listing to 4096 entries, filters import candidates by an explicit extension allow-list and performs filesystem enumeration/metadata IO off the Compose/UI thread. Export destinations reject path separators/control names and remain inside the canonical root. Existing files require explicit overwrite confirmation.
- `CORRECTNESS/ATOMICITY`: shared-storage writes use a same-directory private temporary name, flushed/synced output and atomic replace when the filesystem supports it, with replace-only fallback for filesystems that do not support atomic move. Temporary destination files are cleaned after failure. No partial final export is intentionally exposed.
- `ANALYSIS`: Analysis snapshot import/export retains schema `VulkanScopeAnalysisSnapshot1`, the existing semantic validator and an 8 MiB ceiling. Import is bounded before parse; export validates the exact generated snapshot before atomic write. Selecting a snapshot as baseline retains existing Analysis semantics.
- `PROFILE`: minimum-profile import/export retains schema `VulkanScopeMinimumProfile1`, a 256 KiB ceiling, profile-name/rule-count/rule-length bounds and rejects empty/unsupported content. No profile evaluation semantics are changed.
- `REPORT/JSON`: raw `technicalReport` export is the existing exact schema-v3 `technicalReportJson` serialization, bounded to 8 MiB, and gains only the shared-storage transport. Report schema/content is not modified by this release.
- `REPORT/TXT/HTML`: TXT/HTML export retains the complete-report readiness gate. The exact report/display/driver-mode snapshot is serialized off the main thread into a private cache snapshot at export initiation, bounded to 64 MiB, with at most one pending snapshot. Pending snapshot metadata is saveable across configuration recreation. The chosen shared-storage destination is populated by bounded streaming copy from that private snapshot; cancel/success removes the private snapshot and stale snapshots are bounded/cleaned. This preserves the established exact-snapshot and no-main-thread-filesystem rules without SAF.
- `REGRESSION`: Turnip file-manager/import semantics, installed-driver slots, updater/download validation, Database submission, Vulkan/native/JNI collection, registry/generated artifacts, report schemas, AndroidManifest permissions, API levels, ABI set, resources and dependency pins remain immutable from 1.3.5. Production runtime changes are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt`; `app/build.gradle.kts` changes release identity only.
- `TEST-GAP`: historical gates that require SAF or the 1.3.3/1.3.4 intentionally-unavailable export placeholders are superseded documentary evidence and must not be run to force old behavior back into production.
- `GATES`: `tests/golden/1.3.5_storage_exchange_contract.json`, `tools/verify_release_1306.py`, `tools/test_release_1306_state_machine.py`, `tools/test_release_1306_negative_mutations.py` and `tools/verify_release_1306_regression.py` are the targeted 1.3.6 gates. The verifier must fail immutable 1.3.5 with version checking disabled, pass 1.3.6, reject representative SAF/path/symlink/bounds/permission/atomicity/schema/snapshot-lifecycle regressions and accept an unrelated permitted wording mutation.
- Android compile/lint/unit remain a separate execution class and may be PASS only if Gradle actually starts and completes them. If wrapper/dependency networking prevents execution, record `NOT EXECUTED` rather than PASS.
- Strict package hygiene, deterministic dual-ZIP reproduction, clean extraction, full source-to-extract byte equality and targeted 1.3.6 gate rerun on the clean extract are mandatory. The full historical gate chain is explicitly not required for this storage-only release.

## Release 1.3.8 filter selector and Turnip file-manager Material 3 Expressive refinement
- Application version is 1.3.8 with versionCode 1308. VulkanScope 1.3.7 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `81bc98f8f33db10155bae964b9acbf5f1794dbbbc55f8d0a5976f12243950d87` with 564 files.
- `USER-DIRECTED/USABILITY`: this release changes only the shared single-filter selector and Turnip file-manager presentation requested from real-device screenshots. Vulkan/native/JNI collection, report schemas, Database, updater, storage authority/validation, Turnip archive security, manifest, ABI/API/dependency pins and resources remain predecessor behavior/bytes.
- `FILTER/ANIMATION`: opening/closing the single-filter popup uses bounded Material motion (fade + scale) and the selector chevron animates rotation. Enabling/disabling the selector animates its visual state without making a locked filter interactive.
- `FILTER/SEARCH/LAYOUT`: filter search is case-insensitive, uses the shared Expressive search field, resets to page 1, and the visible result list remains lazy and bounded to 50 entries per page. Filter labels wrap instead of being forcibly clipped to one/two lines. The popup width follows the available selector width up to a bounded maximum and the result list exposes the existing vertical up/down scroll-boundary indicators.
- `FILTER/PAGINATION`: direct page entry accepts only decimal digits whose parsed value is within `1..pageCount`; invalid characters and out-of-range pages never replace the visible page field or active page. Numeric keyboard intent is used. When there are zero/one result pages, previous/next controls and direct page entry remain visible but disabled; no fake extra page is navigable.

Folder names, title, summary, controls and package details must not inherit dark/black text on dark surfaces. Folder yellow and validated-package green remain semantic accents; selection uses the VulkanScope rose/red accent rather than a competing green selection surface.
- `FILE-MANAGER/EXPRESSIVE`: List/Compact becomes a bounded segmented expressive control with selected-state motion/check feedback. Search uses the shared Expressive search field; top/bottom groups use rounded tonal containers and responsive stacked layout where already required. Folder/package rows keep press semantics, bounded wrapping and vertical scroll hints. The package-details dialog uses the same explicit dark VulkanScope palette and exposes the existing scroll indicators.
- Production runtime changes from immutable 1.3.7 are restricted to `app/src/main/java/com/efishell/vulkanscope/MainActivity.kt`; `app/build.gradle.kts` changes release identity only. No resource, manifest, native/JNI, registry/generated or dependency drift is authorized.

## Release 1.3.9 filter popup, export/storage browser and file-manager interaction refinement
- Application version is 1.3.9 with versionCode 1309. VulkanScope 1.3.8 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `53ad9b4fd9cc6087738293553e5bbab3ade37a11e532b7e8058f5a284b3f8641` with 569 files.
- `USER-DIRECTED/FILTER`: the single-filter popup remains mounted/open while live Vulkan collection changes the available label list. Popup/search/page state must not be keyed to the mutable label collection. Its popup position is anchored to the selector bounds rather than a window-relative arbitrary offset, with bounded horizontal/vertical placement.
- `FILTER/BACK/IME`: while filter search owns the IME, Android Back dismisses keyboard/focus first without closing the filter popup. A subsequent Back closes the popup. The popup therefore disables automatic popup-window Back dismissal and keeps explicit Back handling. Outside-click dismissal remains permitted.
- `FILTER/INTERACTION`: filter result selected/pressed treatment is clipped to the same rounded region that is actually clickable; no ripple/selection drawing may escape the action shape. Existing 1.3.8 case-insensitive search, 50-item pagination, strict `1..pageCount` numeric entry, one-page disabled paging and scroll-boundary indicators remain mandatory.
- `REPORT/TXT/HTML`: complete-report TXT and HTML exports are both always rendered as distinct full-width Material 3 Expressive actions; weighted side-by-side `AnimatedContent` layout is forbidden because it can collapse/hide one export action. Existing complete-report readiness, private snapshot, exact serialization and atomic destination-write contracts remain unchanged.
- `STORAGE-BROWSER/EXPRESSIVE`: the in-app import/export browser uses the VulkanScope Material 3 Expressive dark palette with explicit readable content colors, rounded tonal header/description/name/status/footer groups, shared Expressive search, clipped folder/file interactions and scroll-boundary indicators. Folder and file rows must not draw pressed/selected indication outside their clickable shape.
- `STORAGE-BROWSER/TYPE-LOCK`: export file type is determined only by the requested single allowed extension. The editable field contains the base name; the extension is displayed separately and appended by VulkanScope when saving. Destination validation rechecks the exact fixed extension. Users may rename the file but cannot switch the export type from the browser.
- `TURNIP-FILE-MANAGER/VIEWS`: text `List`/`Compact` mode selectors are replaced by semantic icon buttons. Four actual modes are provided: List, Compact, Grid and Details. Each mode is a clipped radio-style action with a content description and selected-state feedback. Grid uses a bounded adaptive lazy grid; Details expands package/folder metadata without changing validation/import behavior.
- `TURNIP-FILE-MANAGER/INTERACTION`: folder cards and Turnip package cards clip interaction indication to their exact rounded action shape. Package cards are themselves selectable actions in addition to the checkbox, so the selected surface corresponds to a clickable region. Info actions remain independently clickable and archive validation/slot limits are unchanged.
- `UI-COPY`: user-facing application copy must describe the current in-app storage workflow directly and must not mention removed/legacy storage picker architecture. Historical audit/rules documents remain immutable documentary evidence and are not user-facing runtime copy.
- `REGRESSION`: Vulkan/native/JNI collection, report schemas/content, Database submission, updater/download validation, storage authority/confinement/atomic-write security, Turnip ZIP validation and installed-driver semantics, manifest permissions, ABI/API/dependency pins and all predecessor resources remain immutable. Selection/import code rechecks the same identity so UI state cannot bypass the block.

- Application version is 1.3.11 with versionCode 1311. VulkanScope 1.3.10 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `e5c85979dca9ffa0737032b9a2c42fc95d9408fa61e8d4f3aaba5b798e800cee` with 584 files.

## Release 1.3.12 filter compactness, shared-storage actions and driver-manager Expressive refinement
- Application version is 1.3.12 with versionCode 1312. VulkanScope 1.3.11 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `10fb87b0d1e21306964a4e158bfcc4d83ffb07bb0a123a103a04475fd6358add` with 589 files.
- `USER-DIRECTED/FILTER/COMPACTNESS`: the shared single-filter popup omits the search field when the available filter catalogue contains fewer than 5 options. Pagination chrome (previous/next actions, direct page entry, page counter and its divider) is rendered only when the filtered result actually spans more than one page. One-page filters therefore do not reserve empty search/paging space.
- `FILTER/MOTION/CLOSE`: popup mounting and visibility are staged so opening/closing visibly uses bounded Material motion instead of appearing in its final state on the first popup frame. A contained VulkanScope-red X action is always available at the leading edge of the popup header; when search is present it is the same visual height as the search control. Existing IME-first Android Back behavior remains intact.
- `FILTER/SELECTION/RESTORE`: re-opening a filter clears stale search text, selects the page containing the currently selected filter and initializes the lazy list at that selected row. Live Vulkan label updates must not reset an already-open popup.
- `FILTER/PAGE-ENTRY`: direct page entry remains numeric and range-bounded, but the text field must accept a temporary empty state so the existing page number can be deleted and replaced from the keyboard. Valid decimal values in `1..pageCount` immediately navigate; invalid/out-of-range values never become active pages. An empty field normalizes back to the active page when focus leaves it.
- `STORAGE-BROWSER/ACTION-BOUNDARY`: every shared-storage import/export browser used by Analysis JSON, profile JSON, technicalReport JSON, TXT and HTML uses informational folder/file cards whose only navigation/selection action is the same contained VulkanScope-red right-arrow control used by the Turnip file manager. Press/ripple feedback is confined to that arrow. Export type locking, canonical-path confinement, bounded scans and atomic writes remain unchanged.
- `DRIVER-MANAGER/EXPRESSIVE`: the System Vulkan driver and managed Turnip slot cards use explicit Material 3 Expressive visual grouping rather than plain key/value text. The System source gets a semantic Android badge, state treatment and tonal evidence pills. Turnip slots get the existing official Mesa badge, explicit state pill, grouped source/version/package evidence and outlined/tonal active/inactive card treatment. Activation/removal/details semantics and driver evidence are unchanged.
- `REGRESSION`: Vulkan/native/JNI collection, registry/generated data, report schemas/content, Database, updater, Turnip archive validation/private schema, storage authority/confinement/serialization, manifest permissions, API/ABI/dependency pins and all packaged resources remain predecessor behavior/bytes. ## Release 1.3.13 filter anchoring, driver branding and Turnip metadata semantics
- Application version is 1.3.13 with versionCode 1313. VulkanScope 1.3.12 is the immutable predecessor; predecessor ZIP SHA-256 is `e68eef88cd73f4b3b095fd7aea768dfc5a75eab3f8af82e99b33a169c48d578d` with 594 files.
- `USER-DIRECTED/TURNIP/BRANDING`: every Mesa/Turnip logo presentation uses the packaged Mesa geometry but renders it with the VulkanScope accent-red tint and matching accent container/border treatment, including file-manager rows/grid/info, managed-driver cards and composite Mesa actions. The packaged Mesa image bytes remain unchanged.
- `USER-DIRECTED/SYSTEM-DRIVER/BRANDING`: the System driver card no longer uses the Android glyph. It resolves the retained System summary vendor ID to the same packaged GPU-vendor logo catalogue used by Overview and presents that logo inside the existing 50 dp VulkanScope-red badge. Unknown/unparseable vendor IDs fail closed to the packaged unknown-vendor logo. System evidence values and activation semantics remain unchanged.
- `BUG/TURNIP/METADATA-LABELS`: package metadata named `driverVersion` is Vulkan-version metadata in the accepted Turnip package schema and must not be presented as a driver-name/version identity field. - `USER-DIRECTED/FILTER/ANCHOR`: the single-filter menu is an in-layout expanding section directly below its selector, never a free popup window that can flip above, overlap the selector or dismiss on outside taps. Expanding the filter increases normal page content height so the owning page remains scrollable. Opening uses bounded fade/scale/vertical Material motion.
- `USER-DIRECTED/FILTER/DISMISS`: while enabled, the filter menu is closed only from its contained red X action. The selector chevron opens but does not close an already-open menu, result selection does not close it, outside/page interaction does not close it, and Android Back only clears an active IME focus while the menu is open. Existing selected-row restore, live-label stability, search threshold, strict editable paging and scroll hints remain intact.
